`live_push.py` driver AFTER `cs1_savnac_desired_course`\nbuilds the (still-placeholder) `DesiredCourse`, mutating the affected PM\nreading page `DesiredObject.body_markdown` in place before the course is\nhanded to `imprint.reconcile.push_course` -- the same \"resolve live links\nright before rendering\" split `cs1_course_map.py`'s `LiveLinkResolver`\nalready established for the Semester Map CSV, applied here to a page body\ninstead of a CSV cell.\n\nIdempotent across reruns: the substitution is deterministic given the same\nlive deck -- same resolved URL every run -> same body_markdown -> same\n`imprint.reconcile` content hash -> `push_course` reports \"skip\" once the\nlink has been pushed once, exactly like any other unchanged object. No\nrepeated page-body thrash, no duplicate link lines -- the placeholder\ntoken is consumed/replaced exactly once per render, never appended to, so\nrunning this twice in a row produces byte-identical output both times.\n\nNever guesses a URL (same \"never guess a URL\" discipline as\n`cs1_course_map.LiveLinkResolver`): if the deck can't be found live yet\n(not pushed, wrong title),
